             .         .        .                     .        .           .              .         .         .                 .        .         .   .    // length_squared is much faster than length() as it avoids a square root.
             .         .        .                     .        .           .              .         .         .                 .        .         .   .    // Use it for comparisons whenever possible.
             .         .        .                     .        .           .              .         .         .                 .        .         .   .    [[nodiscard]] constexpr double length_squared() const noexcept { return x * x + y * y + z * z; }
             .         .        .                     .        .           .              .         .         .                 .        .         .   .

             .         .        .                     .        .           .              .         .         .                 .        .         .   .
